EVITA

| ’PERFECTIVE_PROGRESSIVE’ | ’IMPERFECTIVE_PROGRESSIVE’ | ’NONE’

3.1.2.2.2. Atributele marcajului 
Forma BNF a marcajului TIMEX3:  
attributes:: = tid type (value | valueFromFunction) 
[functionInDocument] [beginPoint] [endPoint] [quant] [freq] 
[temporalFunction] [mod][anchorTimeID] 
tid :: =  TimeID 
TimeID ::= t<integer> 
type :: =  ’DATE’ | ’TIME’ | ’DURATION’ | ’SET’ 
value :: =  CDATA 
{value::= duration | dateTime | time | date | gYearMonth | gYear | 
gMonthDay | gDay | gMonth} 
valueFromFunction:: = IDREF  
{valueFromFunction:: =  TemporalFunctionID 
TemporalFunctionID ::= tf<integer>} 
functionInDocument:: = ’CREATION_TIME’ | ’EXPIRATION_TIME’ | 
’MODIFICATION_TIME’ | ’PUBLICATION_TIME’ | ’RELEASE_TIME’ | 
’RECEPTION_TIME’ | ’NONE’   
beginPoint :: = IDREF {beginPoint :: =  TimeID} \n\n&RQWULEXĠLLODSUHOXFUDUHDOLPELLURPkQHIRORVLQGPHWRGHGHDQDOL]ăDGLVFXUVXOXL 
90 
endPoint :: =  IDREF {endPoint :: =  TimeID} 
quant :: =  CDATA 
freq :: =  CDATA 
temporalFunction :: =  ’true’|’false’  
mod:: = ’BEFORE’ | ’AFTER’ | ’ON_OR_BEFORE’ | ’ON_OR_AFTER’ | 
’LESS_THAN’ |  ’MORE_THAN’ | ’EQUAL_OR_LESS’ | ’EQUAL_OR_MORE’ | 
’START’ | ’MID’ | ’END’ |  ’APPROX’ 
anchorTimeID :: =  IDREF {anchorTimeID :: =  TimeID} 
 
1) tid: atribut obligatoriu, ID-ul expresiei temporale; fiecare expresie TIMEX3 WUHEXLH Vă
ILHLGHQWLILFDWăSULQWU-un ID unic. Acesta este asignat automat. 
2) type:atribut obligatoriu. 
Fiecare TIMEX3 DUHDVLJQDWXQXOGLQXUPăWRDUHOHWLSXUL 
- DATE SHQWUXH[SUHVLLFHGHQRWăGDte calendaristice: the second of December, yesterday, the 
summer of 1971, Tuesday; 
- TIME – momente ale zilei: five minuts past eight; 7:30am; 9:00 am Friday, October 1, 
1755; 
- DURATION – RGXUDWă2 months, 48 hours, all last night, three weeks; 
- SET – pentru captarea expresiilor temporale cuantificate: twice a weak, every two days. 
Valoarea acestui atribut depinde de contextul în care apare expresia. Valorile DATE úLTIME 
SHQWUXWLSXOGHH[SUHVLHWHPSRUDOăSRWILXúRUFRQIXQGDWHvQVăOXkQGvQFRQVLGHUDUHJUDnularitatea

3.1.2.3. Marcajul SIGNAL 
3.1.2.3.1. Criterii de adnotare 
Un signal este un element din text care IDFHH[SOLFLWăUHODĠLDGLQWUHGRXăHQWLWăĠLRH[SUHVLH
WHPSRUDOăúLXQHYHQLPHQWVDXGRXăHYHQLPHQWHLQGLFăIDSWXOFăHYHQLPHQWXOHVWHGHWHUPLQDWGH

eveniment. 
În general, un signal PDUFKHD]ăXQDdintre XUPăWRDUHOHFDWHJRULL 
- 3UHSR]LĠLLWHPSRUDOHla, în, pe, de pe, pâQăSHvQDLQWHGXSăvQWLPSXO etc.; 
- &RQMXQFĠLLWHPSRUDOHvQDLQWHGXSăvQWLPSXOFkWWLPSFkQGetc.; 
- Modificatori temporali: GHGRXăRULGHILHFDUHGDWă etc.; 
- Expresii negative: QXQLFLXQXOQLFLRGDWăQLPHQL etc; 
- Verbe auxiliare modale: a putea, a trebui; 
- 3UHSR]LĠLLVXERUGRQDWRDUHVă 
- Caractere speciale: „-” úL„/” vQ H[SUHVLL WHPSRUDOH FH GHVHPQHD]ă LQWHUYDOH 4-6 
septembrie, aprilie 1999/ iulie 1999). 
'RXăsignal-XUL FDUH DSDU DOăWXUDWHvQWU-R SURSR]LĠLH VXQW PDUFDWH VHSDUDW GRDU GDFă DSDUĠLQ
unor tipuri diferite. Altfel sunt adnotate ca un singur SIGNAL:  
Ei vor investiga rolul pe care l -au avut Statele Unite <SIGNAL sid=”s2”> înainte, în timpul 
úLGXSă </SIGNAL> genocid.

3.1.2.4.2. Atributele marcajului 
Forma BNF a marcajului TLINK este: 
attributes :: =  [lid] [origin] (eventID | timeID) [signalID] 
(relatedtoEvent | relatedtoTime) relType  
lid :: =  LinkID 
LinkID :: =  l<integer> 
origin :: =  CDATA 
eventID :: =  e<integer> 
timeID :: =  t<integer> 
signalID :: =  s<integer> 
relatedToEvent :: =  e<integer> 
relatedToTime :: =  t<integer> 
relType:: =  ‘BEFORE’ | ‘AFTER’ | ‘INCLUDES’ | ‘IS_INCLUDED’ | 
‘DURING’ |  ‘SIMULTANEOUS’ | ‘IAFTER’ | ‘IBEFORE’ | ‘IDENTITY’ | 
‘BEGINS’ | ‘ENDS’ |  ‘BEGUN_BY’  |  ‘ENDED_BY’ 
Atributele includ ID -XO HYHQLPHQWXOXL VXUVă relatedToEvent DO HQWLWăĠLL GHVWLQDĠLH
(eventID WLSXO UHODĠLHL relType úL GDFăUHODĠLD H VHPQDODWă GH XQsignal, ID -ul acestuia 
(signalID).

continue well into the future if he doesn't stop

<!ELEMENT TimeML ( #PCDATA | ALINK | CONFIDENCE | EVENT | MAKEINSTANCE | 
SIGNAL | SLINK | TIMEX3 | TLINK )* > 
<!ATTLIST TimeML xsi:noNamespaceSchemaLocation CDATA #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST TimeML xmlns:xsi CDATA #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST TimeML comment CDATA #IMPLIED > 
 
<!ELEMENT EVENT ( #PCDATA ) > 
<!ATTLIST EVENT eid ID #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST EVENT class ( ASPECTUAL | I_ACTION | I_STATE | OCCURRENCE | 
PERCEPTION | REPORTING | STATE ) #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST EVENT stem CDATA #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST EVENT comment CDATA #IMPLIED > 
 
<!ELEMENT MAKEINSTANCE EMPTY > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE eiid ID #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE eventID IDREF #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE signalID IDREF #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE pos ( ADJECTIVE | NOUN | VERB | PREPOSITION | NONE ) 
#REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE tense ( FUTURE | INFINITIVE | NONE | PAST | PASTPART | 
PRESENT | PRESPART ) #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE aspect ( NONE | PERFECTIVE | PERFECTIVE_PROGRESSIVE | 
PROGRESSIVE ) #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE cardinality CDATA #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE polarity ( POS | NEG ) #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE modality CDATA #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST MAKEINSTANCE comment CDATA #IMPLIED > 
 
<!ELEMENT TIMEX3 ( #PCDATA ) > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 tid ID #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 type ( DATE | DURATION | SET | TIME ) #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 value NMTOKEN #REQUIRED > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 anchorTimeID IDREF #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 beginPoint IDREF #IMPLIED > 
<!ATTLIST TIMEX3 endPoint IDREF #IMPLIED >
